- **axiom-reduce**: Find the smallest set of axioms that forces a result, and use it to improve the result -- merge duplicate axioms, settle open conventions, and state each result's exact dependencies. Use after a result is verified and before connecting it to other theorems ("first check what axioms can improve it"). Worked example: grid81_operators_applied.py -- seven results reduce to A1 (10 = 1 mod 9), A2 (3 | 9), A3 (primes &gt; 3 are coprime to 3); A1 merged the "sum collapse" and "mod-9 collapse" axioms and the grid's 1..9 column index fixed the mod-9 representative convention. ([CylicAmp/CylicAmp](https://github.com/CylicAmp/CylicAmp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/axiom-reduce))
